000099sec_1STANDOC_48 (generic warning) Hanging paragraph in clause
<clause id="_6f272aa4-ebca-6aec-eabe-e1681b2760f6" anchor="sec_1" obligation="normative">
<title id="_6a13218d-655a-ce5a-a75b-2a8f4a2affc3">Overview</title>
<clause id="_e3bf85c9-9f35-9245-2ed1-e7071de2499c" anchor="sec_1.1" obligation="normative">
<title id="_c9cbec3b-d4ec-14ad-da82-46f2c501d9c9">Preface</title>
<p id="_95b930f2-4dc0-30ce-ba13-f10503df3420">The “Data Classification and Encoding Guide” has been developed to provide consistent, standardized instructions for encoding S-100 compliant Marine Radio Services (MRS) (S-123) data.</p>
2
000199_9a14f81e-36f1-0a46-fc87-8c9d2c6141c6STANDOC_48 (generic warning) Hanging paragraph in clause
<clause id="_9a14f81e-36f1-0a46-fc87-8c9d2c6141c6" obligation="normative">
<title id="_0427c304-cf37-9e07-b49c-c924814e0f07">Feature</title>
<p id="_06d11aaf-acbf-21a6-421f-f2e550aaf2e0">A feature contains descriptive attributes that characterize real world entities.</p>

<p id="_9aeece56-0f5a-8847-36f7-01339fdc2d43">The word ‘feature’ as used in the ISO 191xx series and in S-100 based product specifications has two distinct but related senses — ’feature type’ and ‘feature instance’. A feature instance is a single occurrence of the feature and is represented as an object in a dataset.</p>
2
000327_d381e473-d43c-d197-7058-55ad53be0445STANDOC_48 (generic warning) Hanging paragraph in clause
<clause id="_d381e473-d43c-d197-7058-55ad53be0445" obligation="normative">
<title id="_777cfd87-252f-8149-535b-c6397e038d9d">Attributes</title>
<p id="_62a11130-d3b6-7d52-9a5d-eaaf6688f067">Attributes may be simple type or complex type. Complex (C) attributes are aggregates of other attributes that can be simple type or complex type attributes. Simple (S) attributes are assigned to one of the types collected at  <xref target="sec_2.4.1"/>.</p>

<p id="_15d9ee84-ccc3-fa04-ecab-caad8c68b768">The binding of attributes to a feature, the binding of attributes to attributes to construct complex attributes, and attribute multiplicity are all defined in the Feature Catalogue.</p>
2
000503_a05678ed-1b52-d491-6603-092da00a7a16STANDOC_48 (generic warning) Hanging paragraph in clause
<clause id="_a05678ed-1b52-d491-6603-092da00a7a16" obligation="normative">
<title id="_3f1e5a70-07ea-b431-8208-233ae3d65497">Spatial attribute types</title>
<p id="_8ff584cc-1e6a-3057-4094-c87fb3ae0fe0">Spatial attribute types must contain a referenced geometry and may be associated with spatial quality attributes. Each spatial attribute instance must be referenced by a feature instance or another spatial attribute instance.</p>

<clause id="_090145c4-1485-6604-4238-c62934538801" obligation="normative">
2
000551_2100e0c3-6f06-7bee-3737-7bfaebda97d3STANDOC_48 (generic warning) Hanging paragraph in clause
<clause id="_2100e0c3-6f06-7bee-3737-7bfaebda97d3" obligation="normative">
<title id="_3628d182-a45a-885a-04c7-a8965475fe29">Textual information</title>
<p id="_b1178514-2572-2230-8e94-ec9a6cc9d45f">Textual information may provide additional information essential to understand the presence of the MRS and other features of an S-123 product. This information may also provide legal information pertaining to the S-123 product features.</p>

<p id="_79d15c65-7dde-af76-efbf-786743b805b4">The methods to provide textual information vary from the simple provision of short text, to the more structured provision of extensive text. The length of the text determines the method and the attribute selection, see  <xref target="sec_2.4.8.2"/>.</p>
2
000745_1cb56f92-52a2-2f32-738d-a090f41fd273STANDOC_48 (generic warning) Hanging paragraph in clause
<clause id="_1cb56f92-52a2-2f32-738d-a090f41fd273" obligation="normative">
<title id="_d151fb11-46a4-e80a-d34b-90e47e6d0cf2">Dates</title>
<p id="_fbdcd398-f2d8-3844-4caf-fd39f424e094">Dates may be need to be encoded as complete or truncated values, depending on available information and allowed format for the particular attribute. The definition of the attribute will indicate if it must take a complete value (type  <em>Date</em> or <em>DA</em>) or is allowed to take a truncated value (type  <em>S100_TruncatedDate</em> or <em>TD</em>). Complete and truncated dates are different value types (see  <eref type="inline" bibitemid="S100" citeas="S-100"> <localityStack> <locality type="clause"> <referenceFrom>1</referenceFrom> <referenceTo>4.5.2</referenceTo> </locality> <locality type="table"> <referenceFrom>1</referenceFrom> <referenceTo>2</referenceTo> </locality> </localityStack> </eref>).</p>

<p id="_9221d481-e9a9-8b47-9819-d3c1e84835f8">For attributes that use the complete date type (type <em>Date</em> or <em>DA</em>), all their components (year, month, and day) must be specified.</p>
2
000885sec_​2.4.12STANDOC_48 (generic warning) Hanging paragraph in clause
<clause id="_99e3a61a-b3cd-33d1-380e-3b5b51e00eab" anchor="sec_2.4.12" obligation="normative">
<title id="_ec3f351a-4c68-277d-0ba3-1121fade6732">Graphic information</title>
<p id="_7b4f22d5-5a9d-e3aa-3692-3e86fd112a36">A graphic file should be appropriate for the purpose and should supplement the information in terms of navigational relevance. Preferably, the graphic should provide perspective relevant to the view of the mariner. Graphics should be such that all the information in the graphic is legible in the application display.</p>

<p id="_a5c5400c-7591-9bb9-c1f5-e993bb124fcd">Graphic information must be encoded using the complex attribute <strong>graphic</strong>. The simple sub-attribute  <strong>pictureInformation</strong> should be used to provide credits to the picture creator, copyright owner etc.</p>
2
000940sec_2.5.4STANDOC_48 (generic warning) Hanging paragraph in clause
<clause id="_c922d886-3a3b-f467-67b4-d3b7bb853bfe" anchor="sec_2.5.4" obligation="normative">
<title id="_5e667ac4-5169-42ff-2951-9ae5d2551018">Association classes</title>
<p id="_5d7df52b-63f2-74c5-9b1d-a30bb8bfe6e3">Association classes allow relationships to be characterized by one or more attributes. The attributes of the association class belong to the association itself, not to any of the features or information types it connects. An association class is both an association and a class. Within an S-123 product the association classes <strong>Permission Type</strong> and <strong>Inclusion Type</strong> may be used for relating vessel classes to feature and information types.</p>

<clause id="_479f0050-c295-f0dd-e07c-fa0782788018" obligation="normative">
2
001170_f9c999b1-c836-041b-66f3-f59f67730389STANDOC_48 (generic warning) Hanging paragraph in clause
<clause id="_f9c999b1-c836-041b-66f3-f59f67730389" obligation="normative">
<title id="_51a632ed-ad09-ef79-c064-719f28a8111b">Masking</title>
<p id="_be153fbc-a235-0c10-8c03-c5568b46811a">To improve the look and feel of the display of MRSs in ECDIS for the mariner certain features, or certain edges of features, should be masked.</p>

<clause id="_386c23fa-bc56-053f-2efd-8b7599c60c46" obligation="normative">

001633sec_5STANDOC_48 (generic warning) Hanging paragraph in clause
<clause id="_f089b9e7-8260-a02b-1c03-e16099d15a41" anchor="sec_5" obligation="normative">
<title id="_dfe74311-ea47-a88f-850b-ea7442fdfd55">Geographic Features</title>
<p id="_5565c1b2-6c27-630d-29fa-e488ee41ecf4">This section describes abstract as well as non-abstract types. The abstract type  <strong>FeatureType</strong> cannot be used directly, but defines attributes inherited by its sub-types. The encoding remarks in the description of  <strong>FeatureType</strong> apply to its sub-types but may be overridden by remarks in the sub-type.</p>

<clause id="_8b9fcd89-e59c-71fb-e129-510578a6b730" anchor="sec_5.1" obligation="normative">
2

<table id="_765127b5-7da1-2b07-0825-b2b28141585f" width="100%"> <tbody> <tr id="_cbdd33c6-91db-4373-b085-7cfbab3b5a2c"> <td id="_269beb46-b5cc-53fb-d0bb-4d26b3f89516" valign="top" align="left"> <p id="_64031242-f5fb-418e-5363-433d68fbb046"> <underline>IHO Definition:</underline> The Maritime Mobile Service Identity (MMSI) Code is formed of a series of nine digits which are transmitted over the radio path in order to uniquely identify ship stations, ship earth stations, coast stations, coast earth stations, and group calls. These identities are formed in such a way that the identity or part thereof can be used by telephone and telex subscribers connected to the general telecommunications network principally to call ships automatically.</p>

<p id="_81c8549b-63e5-5efd-a5ac-852a9ab4f8a6"> <underline>Remarks:</underline> </p>

<p id="_3ff255ad-f038-5494-81e1-526ec59d71e8">MMSI code is a 9-digit identity. The assignment of the identities in the maritime mobile service follows the Recommendation ITU-R M.585. The MMSI code to be assigned to a coast station or station located on land starts with 00, while the identity to be assigned to automatic identification systems aids to navigation (AIS AtoN) starts with 99.</p>

007231_d42e9053-09b5-34f7-807e-68199acc2ac6STANDOC_48 (generic warning) Table should have title
<table id="_d42e9053-09b5-34f7-807e-68199acc2ac6" width="100%"> <tbody> <tr id="_bc614be4-59a6-0370-0afe-ed3a6e225551"> <td id="_13d36937-6f90-4b2a-f2c9-a72eb56076aa" valign="top" align="left"> <p id="_185d8ac5-9b03-bd19-39b7-65fc290bd667"> <underline>IHO Definition:</underline> A sector is the part of a circle between two straight lines drawn from the centre to the circumference. Sector bearing specifies the limit of the sector.</p>

<p id="_1bd2e53e-1364-409c-dec6-b9dd4c2dd244"> <underline>Remarks:</underline> </p>

<p id="_66488b6f-6e8c-0bbc-9778-d150058e370b">The values given to the common limits of adjacent sectors should be identical. The orientation of bearing is from seaward to the central object. This conforms with the method used in ‘List of Lights’ publications. A generic term such as ‘to shore’ cannot be used; a specific bearing must be encoded. Where a sector limit is defined as ‘to the shore’, it should be encoded using a value that ensures that, when the limit is drawn, it will fall entirely on land.</p>
